Built on a sparse Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture, it selectively activates only 11B of its 196B parameters per token. This \"intelligence density\" allows it to rival the reasoning depth of top-tier proprietary models, while maintaining the agility required for real-time interaction.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv Key Capabilities\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-2-key-capabilities\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2. Key Capabilities\" href=\"#2-key-capabilities\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDeep Reasoning at Speed\u003c/strong\u003e: While chatbots are built for reading, agents must reason fast. Powered by 3-way Multi-Token Prediction (MTP-3), Step 3.5 Flash achieves a generation throughput of \u003cstrong\u003e100–300 tok/s\u003c/strong\u003e in typical usage (peaking at \u003cstrong\u003e350 tok/s\u003c/strong\u003e for single-stream coding tasks). This allows for complex, multi-step reasoning chains with immediate responsiveness.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eA Robust Engine for Coding \u0026amp; Agents\u003c/strong\u003e: Step 3.5 Flash is purpose-built for agentic tasks, integrating a scalable RL framework that drives consistent self-improvement. It achieves \u003cstrong\u003e74.4% on SWE-bench Verified\u003c/strong\u003e and \u003cstrong\u003e51.0% on Terminal-Bench 2.0\u003c/strong\u003e, proving its ability to handle sophisticated, long-horizon tasks with unwavering stability.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEfficient Long Context\u003c/strong\u003e: The model supports a cost-efficient \u003cstrong\u003e256K context window\u003c/strong\u003e by employing a 3:1 Sliding Window Attention (SWA) ratio—integrating three SWA layers for every full-attention layer. This hybrid approach ensures consistent performance across massive datasets or long codebases while significantly reducing the computational overhead typical of standard long-context models.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eAccessible Local Deployment\u003c/strong\u003e: Optimized for accessibility, Step 3.5 Flash brings elite-level intelligence to local environments. It runs securely on high-end consumer hardware (e.g., Mac Studio M4 Max, NVIDIA DGX Spark), ensuring data privacy without sacrificing performance.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e3. Open-source models (left) are sorted by their total parameter count, while top-tier proprietary models are shown on the right. xbench-DeepSearch scores are sourced from \u003ca href=\"https://xbench.org/agi/aisearch\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eofficial publications\u003c/a\u003e for consistency. The shadowed bars represent the enhanced performance of Step 3.5 Flash using \u003ca href=\"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2601.05593\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003eParallel ensure fair comparability.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBrowseComp (with Context Manager): when the effective context length exceeds a predefined threshold, the agent resets the context and restarts the agent loop. (By contrast, Kimi K2.5 and DeepSeek-V3.2 used a discard-all strategy.)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIn decoding cost section, decoding is estimated using a similar but more accurate approach than \u003ca href=\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19427\" rel=\"nofollow\"\u003earxiv.org/abs/2507.19427\u003c/a\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e4. tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e4.2 Mixture of Experts (MoE) Routing\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-42-mixture-of-experts-moe-routing\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 4.2 Mixture of Experts (MoE) Routing\" href=\"#42-mixture-of-experts-moe-routing\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUnlike traditional dense models, Step 3.5 Flash uses a fine-grained routing strategy to maximize efficiency:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eFine-Grained Experts\u003c/strong\u003e: 288 routed experts per layer + 1 shared expert (always active).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSparse Activation\u003c/strong\u003e: Only the Top-8 experts are selected per token.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eResult\u003c/strong\u003e: The model retains the \"memory\" of a 196B parameter model but executes with the speed of an 11B model.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e4.3 Multi-Token Prediction (MTP)\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-43-multi-token-prediction-mtp\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 4.3 Multi-Token Prediction (MTP)\" href=\"#43-multi-token-prediction-mtp\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eTo improve inference speed, we utilize a specialized MTP Head consisting of a sliding-window attention mechanism and a dense Feed-Forward Network (FFN). This module predicts 4 tokens simultaneously in a single forward pass, significantly accelerating inference without degrading quality.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e5. \u0026quot;your-stepfun-api-key\u0026quot;\nmodel = \u0026quot;step-3.5-flash\u0026quot;\n\nclient = OpenAI(api_key=api_key, base_url=base_url)\n\ncompletion = client.chat.completions.create(\n    model=model,\n    messages=[\n        {\u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;system\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;You are a helpful assistant.\u0026quot;},\n        {\u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;user\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;Hello! Introduce yourself.\u0026quot;}\n    ]\n)\n\nprint(completion.choices[0].message.content)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNote\u003c/strong\u003e: Bug fixes for tool parser and reasoning parser, as well as support for the \u003ccode\u003ev1/messages\u003c/code\u003e interface, are being merged into vLLM. In the meantime, you can use the \u003ccode\u003evllm/vllm-openai:v0.15.1-x86_64\u003c/code\u003e image with \u003ccode\u003estep3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.patch\u003c/code\u003e for deployment. The patch file \u003ccode\u003estep3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.patch\u003c/code\u003e is provided in this repository; download or copy it and ensure it is accessible from the directory where you run \u003ccode\u003egit apply\u003c/code\u003e (or use its absolute path).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"# via Docker\n# refer to \u0026quot;step3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.Dockerfile\u0026quot;\n\n# or via pip \npip install -U vllm==0.15.1\ncd /path/to/lib/python3.12/site-packages  # Replace with the parent dir of vLLM installation path.\ngit apply /path/to/step3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.patch  # Replace with the actual path to the patch file (e.g., from the repository root).\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e via Docker\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e refer to \"step3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.Dockerfile\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e or via pip \u003c/span\u003e\npip install -U vllm==0.15.1\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003ecd\u003c/span\u003e /path/to/lib/python3.12/site-packages  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Replace with the parent dir of vLLM installation path.\u003c/span\u003e\ngit apply /path/to/step3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.patch  \u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e#\u003c/span\u003e Replace with the actual path to the patch file (e.g., from the repository root).\u003c/span\u003e\u003c/pre\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003col start=\"2\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eLaunch the server.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eNote\u003c/strong\u003e: Full MTP3 support is not yet available in vLLM. snippet for quick functional verification. For high-throughput serving, use vLLM or SGLang.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto\" dir=\"auto\" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content=\"from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\n\nMODEL_PATH = \u0026quot;\u0026lt;MODEL_PATH_OR_HF_ID\u0026gt;\u0026quot;\n\n# 1. Setup\ntokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)\nmodel = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\n    MODEL_PATH,\n    trust_remote_code=True,\n    torch_dtype=\u0026quot;auto\u0026quot;,\n    device_map=\u0026quot;auto\u0026quot;,\n)\n\n# 2. Prepare Input\nmessages = [{\u0026quot;role\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;user\u0026quot;, \u0026quot;content\u0026quot;: \u0026quot;Explain the significance of the number 42.\u0026quot;}]\ninputs = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(\n    messages,\n    tokenize=True,\n    add_generation_prompt=True,\n    return_dict=True,\n    return_tensors=\u0026quot;pt\u0026quot;,\n).to(model.device)\n\n# 3. Generate\ngenerated_ids = model.generate(**inputs, max_new_tokens=128, do_sample=False)\noutput_text = tokenizer.decode(generated_ids[0][inputs.input_ids.shape[1]:], skip_special_tokens=True)\n\nprint(output_text)\"\u003e\u003cpre\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003efrom\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etransformers\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-k\"\u003eimport\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eAutoModelForCausalLM\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eAutoTokenizer\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eMODEL_PATH\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"\u0026lt;MODEL_PATH_OR_HF_ID\u0026gt;\"\u003c/span\u003e\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e# 1. Setup\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etokenizer\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eAutoTokenizer\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003efrom_pretrained\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eMODEL_PATH\u003c/span\u003e)\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emodel\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-v\"\u003eAutoModelForCausalLM\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003efrom_pretrained\u003c/span\u003e(\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eMODEL_PATH\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etrust_remote_code\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eTrue\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etorch_dtype\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"auto\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003edevice_map\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"auto\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n)\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e# 2. Prepare Input\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emessages\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e [{\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"role\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"user\"\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"content\"\u003c/span\u003e: \u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"Explain the significance of the number 42.\"\u003c/span\u003e}]\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003einputs\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etokenizer\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eapply_chat_template\u003c/span\u003e(\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emessages\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003etokenize\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eTrue\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003eadd_generation_prompt\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eTrue\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ereturn_dict\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eTrue\u003c/span\u003e,\n    \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003ereturn_tensors\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s\"\u003e\"pt\"\u003c/span\u003e,\n).\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003eto\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emodel\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003edevice\u003c/span\u003e)\n\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-c\"\u003e# 3. Generate\u003c/span\u003e\n\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003egenerated_ids\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emodel\u003c/span\u003e.\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003egenerate\u003c/span\u003e(\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e**\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003einputs\u003c/span\u003e, \u003cspan class=\"pl-s1\"\u003emax_new_tokens\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan class=\"pl-c1\"\u003e=\u003c/span\u003e\u003cspan dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eInstall:\u003c/strong\u003e \u003ccode\u003ecurl -fsSL https://openclaw.ai/install.sh | onboard\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eConfigure:\u003c/strong\u003e In WebUI (\u003ccode\u003eConfig\u003c/code\u003e -\u0026gt; Known Issues and Future Directions\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-9-known-issues-and-future-directions\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 9. Known Issues and Future Directions\" href=\"#9-known-issues-and-future-directions\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eToken Efficiency\u003c/strong\u003e. Step 3.5 Flash achieves frontier-level agentic intelligence but currently relies on longer generation trajectories than Gemini 3.0 Pro to reach comparable quality.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEfficient Universal Mastery\u003c/strong\u003e. We aim to unify generalist versatility with deep domain expertise. To achieve this efficiently, we are advancing variants of on-policy distillation, allowing the model to internalize expert behaviors with higher sample efficiency.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRL for More Agentic Tasks\u003c/strong\u003e. While Step 3.5 Flash demonstrates competitive performance on academic agentic benchmarks, the next frontier of agentic AI necessitates the application of RL to intricate, expert-level tasks found in professional work, engineering, and research.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eOperational Scope and Constraints\u003c/strong\u003e. Step 3.5 Flash is tailored for coding and work-centric tasks, but may experience reduced stability during distribution shifts. This typically occurs in highly specialized domains or long-horizon, multi-turn dialogues, where the model may exhibit repetitive reasoning, mixed-language outputs, or inconsistencies in time and identity awareness.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e10. Co-Developing the Future\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-10-co-developing-the-future\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 10. Co-Developing the Future\" href=\"#10-co-developing-the-future\"\u003e\u003csvg data-component=\"Octicon\" class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWe view our roadmap as a living document, evolving continuously based on real-world usage and developer feedback.\nAs we work to shape the future of AGI by expanding broad model capabilities, we want to ensure we are solving the right problems. We invite you to be part of this continuous feedback loop—your insights directly influence our priorities.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eJoin the Conversation\u003c/strong\u003e: Our Discord community is the primary hub for brainstorming future architectures, proposing capabilities, and getting early access updates 🚀\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eReport Friction\u003c/strong\u003e: Encountering limitations? You can open an issue on GitHub or flag it directly in our Discord support channels.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" <div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1. Introduction</h2><a id="user-content-1-introduction" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1. Introduction" href="#1-introduction"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Step 3.5 Flash</strong> (<a href="https://static.stepfun.com/blog/step-3.5-flash/" rel="nofollow">visit website</a>) is our most capable open-source foundation model, engineered to deliver frontier reasoning and agentic capabilities with exceptional efficiency. Built on a sparse Mixture of Experts (MoE) architecture, it selectively activates only 11B of its 196B parameters per token. This "intelligence density" allows it to rival the reasoning depth of top-tier proprietary models, while maintaining the agility required for real-time interaction.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2. Key Capabilities</h2><a id="user-content-2-key-capabilities" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2. Key Capabilities" href="#2-key-capabilities"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Deep Reasoning at Speed</strong>: While chatbots are built for reading, agents must reason fast. Powered by 3-way Multi-Token Prediction (MTP-3), Step 3.5 Flash achieves a generation throughput of <strong>100–300 tok/s</strong> in typical usage (peaking at <strong>350 tok/s</strong> for single-stream coding tasks). This allows for complex, multi-step reasoning chains with immediate responsiveness.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>A Robust Engine for Coding &amp; Agents</strong>: Step 3.5 Flash is purpose-built for agentic tasks, integrating a scalable RL framework that drives consistent self-improvement. It achieves <strong>74.4% on SWE-bench Verified</strong> and <strong>51.0% on Terminal-Bench 2.0</strong>, proving its ability to handle sophisticated, long-horizon tasks with unwavering stability.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Efficient Long Context</strong>: The model supports a cost-efficient <strong>256K context window</strong> by employing a 3:1 Sliding Window Attention (SWA) ratio—integrating three SWA layers for every full-attention layer. This hybrid approach ensures consistent performance across massive datasets or long codebases while significantly reducing the computational overhead typical of standard long-context models.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Accessible Local Deployment</strong>: Optimized for accessibility, Step 3.5 Flash brings elite-level intelligence to local environments. It runs securely on high-end consumer hardware (e.g., Mac Studio M4 Max, NVIDIA DGX Spark), ensuring data privacy without sacrificing performance.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3. Performance</h2><a id="user-content-3-performance" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3. Performance" href="#3-performance"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Step 3.5 Flash delivers performance parity with leading closed-source systems while remaining open and efficient.</p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" href="/stepfun-ai/Step-3.5-Flash/blob/main/assets/step-bar-chart.png"><img src="/stepfun-ai/Step-3.5-Flash/raw/main/assets/step-bar-chart.png" alt="" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">Performance of Step 3.5 Flash measured across <strong>Reasoning</strong>, <strong>Coding</strong>, and <strong>Agentic Capabilities</strong>. Open-source models (left) are sorted by their total parameter count, while top-tier proprietary models are shown on the right. xbench-DeepSearch scores are sourced from <a href="https://xbench.org/agi/aisearch" rel="nofollow">official publications</a> for consistency. The shadowed bars represent the enhanced performance of Step 3.5 Flash using <a href="https://arxiv.org/pdf/2601.05593" rel="nofollow">Parallel Thinking</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Notes</h2><a id="user-content-notes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Notes" href="#notes"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>"—" indicates the score is not publicly available or not tested.</li>
<li>"*" indicates the original score was inaccessible or lower than our reproduced, so we report the evaluation under the same test conditions as Step 3.5 Flash to ensure fair comparability.</li>
<li>BrowseComp (with Context Manager): when the effective context length exceeds a predefined threshold, the agent resets the context and restarts the agent loop. (By contrast, Kimi K2.5 and DeepSeek-V3.2 used a discard-all strategy.)</li>
<li>In decoding cost section, decoding is estimated using a similar but more accurate approach than <a href="https://arxiv.org/abs/2507.19427" rel="nofollow">arxiv.org/abs/2507.19427</a>.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4. Architecture Details</h2><a id="user-content-4-architecture-details" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4. Architecture Details" href="#4-architecture-details"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Step 3.5 Flash is built on a <strong>Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)</strong> transformer architecture, optimized for high throughput and low VRAM usage during inference.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4.1 Technical Specifications</h3><a id="user-content-41-technical-specifications" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4.1 Technical Specifications" href="#41-technical-specifications"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<markdown-accessiblity-table><table>
<thead>
<tr>
<th align="left">Component</th>
<th align="left">Specification</th>
</tr>
</thead>
<tbody>
<tr>
<td align="left"><strong>Backbone</strong></td>
<td align="left">45-layer Transformer (4,096 hidden dim)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td align="left"><strong>Context Window</strong></td>
<td align="left">256K</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td align="left"><strong>Vocabulary</strong></td>
<td align="left">128,896 tokens</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td align="left"><strong>Total Parameters</strong></td>
<td align="left"><strong>196.81B</strong> (196B Backbone + 0.81B Head)</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td align="left"><strong>Active Parameters</strong></td>
<td align="left"><strong>~11B</strong> (per token generation)</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table></markdown-accessiblity-table>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4.2 Mixture of Experts (MoE) Routing</h3><a id="user-content-42-mixture-of-experts-moe-routing" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4.2 Mixture of Experts (MoE) Routing" href="#42-mixture-of-experts-moe-routing"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Unlike traditional dense models, Step 3.5 Flash uses a fine-grained routing strategy to maximize efficiency:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Fine-Grained Experts</strong>: 288 routed experts per layer + 1 shared expert (always active).</li>
<li><strong>Sparse Activation</strong>: Only the Top-8 experts are selected per token.</li>
<li><strong>Result</strong>: The model retains the "memory" of a 196B parameter model but executes with the speed of an 11B model.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4.3 Multi-Token Prediction (MTP)</h3><a id="user-content-43-multi-token-prediction-mtp" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4.3 Multi-Token Prediction (MTP)" href="#43-multi-token-prediction-mtp"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To improve inference speed, we utilize a specialized MTP Head consisting of a sliding-window attention mechanism and a dense Feed-Forward Network (FFN). This module predicts 4 tokens simultaneously in a single forward pass, significantly accelerating inference without degrading quality.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">5. Quick Start</h2><a id="user-content-5-quick-start" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 5. Quick Start" href="#5-quick-start"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can get started with Step 3.5 Flash in minutes using Cloud API via our supported providers.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">5.4 Implementation Example</h3><a id="user-content-54-implementation-example" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 5.4 Implementation Example" href="#54-implementation-example"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Install the standard OpenAI SDK (compatible with both platforms):</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="pip install --upgrade &quot;openai&gt;=1.0&quot;"><pre>pip install --upgrade <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>openai&gt;=1.0<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Python Example:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from openai import OpenAI

# Configuration for OpenRouter
# base_url = &quot;https://openrouter.ai/api/v1&quot;
# api_key = &quot;sk-or-...&quot; 
# model = &quot;stepfun/step-3.5-flash&quot;

# Configuration for StepFun (International)
base_url = &quot;https://api.stepfun.ai/v1&quot; 
# For China: base_url = &quot;https://api.stepfun.com/v1&quot;
api_key = &quot;your-stepfun-api-key&quot;
model = &quot;step-3.5-flash&quot;

client = OpenAI(api_key=api_key, base_url=base_url)

completion = client.chat.completions.create(
    model=model,
    messages=[
        {&quot;role&quot;: &quot;system&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;You are a helpful assistant.&quot;},
        {&quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Hello! Introduce yourself.&quot;}
    ]
)

print(completion.choices[0].message.content)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">openai</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">OpenAI</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Configuration for OpenRouter</span>
<span class="pl-c"># base_url = "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1"</span>
<span class="pl-c"># api_key = "sk-or-..." </span>
<span class="pl-c"># model = "stepfun/step-3.5-flash"</span>

<span class="pl-c"># Configuration for StepFun (International)</span>
<span class="pl-s1">base_url</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"https://api.stepfun.ai/v1"</span> 
<span class="pl-c"># For China: base_url = "https://api.stepfun.com/v1"</span>
<span class="pl-s1">api_key</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"your-stepfun-api-key"</span>
<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"step-3.5-flash"</span>

<span class="pl-s1">client</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">OpenAI</span>(<span class="pl-s1">api_key</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">api_key</span>, <span class="pl-s1">base_url</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">base_url</span>)

<span class="pl-s1">completion</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">client</span>.<span class="pl-c1">chat</span>.<span class="pl-c1">completions</span>.<span class="pl-c1">create</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">model</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s1">model</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">messages</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span>[
        {<span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"system"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"You are a helpful assistant."</span>},
        {<span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Hello! Introduce yourself."</span>}
    ]
)

<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">completion</span>.<span class="pl-c1">choices</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>].<span class="pl-c1">message</span>.<span class="pl-c1">content</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6. Local Deployment</h2><a id="user-content-6-local-deployment" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6. Local Deployment" href="#6-local-deployment"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Step 3.5 Flash is optimized for local inference and supports industry-standard backends including vLLM, SGLang, Hugging Face Transformers and llama.cpp.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.1 vLLM</h3><a id="user-content-61-vllm" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.1 vLLM" href="#61-vllm"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We recommend using the latest nightly build of vLLM.</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Install vLLM.</li>
</ol>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# via Docker
docker pull vllm/vllm-openai:nightly

# or via pip (nightly wheels)
pip install -U vllm --pre \
  --index-url https://pypi.org/simple \
  --extra-index-url https://wheels.vllm.ai/nightly"><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> via Docker</span>
docker pull vllm/vllm-openai:nightly

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> or via pip (nightly wheels)</span>
pip install -U vllm --pre \
  --index-url https://pypi.org/simple \
  --extra-index-url https://wheels.vllm.ai/nightly</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note</strong>: Bug fixes for tool parser and reasoning parser, as well as support for the <code>v1/messages</code> interface, are being merged into vLLM. In the meantime, you can use the <code>vllm/vllm-openai:v0.15.1-x86_64</code> image with <code>step3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.patch</code> for deployment. The patch file <code>step3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.patch</code> is provided in this repository; download or copy it and ensure it is accessible from the directory where you run <code>git apply</code> (or use its absolute path).</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# via Docker
# refer to &quot;step3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.Dockerfile&quot;

# or via pip 
pip install -U vllm==0.15.1
cd /path/to/lib/python3.12/site-packages  # Replace with the parent dir of vLLM installation path.
git apply /path/to/step3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.patch  # Replace with the actual path to the patch file (e.g., from the repository root)."><pre><span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> via Docker</span>
<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> refer to "step3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.Dockerfile"</span>

<span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> or via pip </span>
pip install -U vllm==0.15.1
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> /path/to/lib/python3.12/site-packages  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Replace with the parent dir of vLLM installation path.</span>
git apply /path/to/step3.5_vllm_v0.15.1.patch  <span class="pl-c"><span class="pl-c">#</span> Replace with the actual path to the patch file (e.g., from the repository root).</span></pre></div>
<ol start="2" dir="auto">
<li>Launch the server.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note</strong>: Full MTP3 support is not yet available in vLLM. We are actively working on a Pull Request to integrate this feature, which is expected to significantly enhance decoding performance.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>For fp8 model</li>
</ul>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="vllm serve &lt;MODEL_PATH_OR_HF_ID&gt; \
  --served-model-name step3p5-flash \
  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \
  --enable-expert-parallel \
  --disable-cascade-attn \
  --reasoning-parser step3p5 \
  --enable-auto-tool-choice \
  --tool-call-parser step3p5 \
  --hf-overrides '{&quot;num_nextn_predict_layers&quot;: 1}' \
  --speculative_config '{&quot;method&quot;: &quot;step3p5_mtp&quot;, &quot;num_speculative_tokens&quot;: 1}' \
  --trust-remote-code \
  --quantization fp8"><pre>vllm serve <span class="pl-k">&lt;</span>MODEL_PATH_OR_HF_ID<span class="pl-k">&gt;</span> \
  --served-model-name step3p5-flash \
  --tensor-parallel-size 8 \
  --enable-expert-parallel \
  --disable-cascade-attn \
  --reasoning-parser step3p5 \
  --enable-auto-tool-choice \
  --tool-call-parser step3p5 \
  --hf-overrides <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>{"num_nextn_predict_layers": 1}<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> \
  --speculative_config <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">'</span>{"method": "step3p5_mtp", "num_speculative_tokens": 1}<span class="pl-pds">'</span></span> \
  --trust-remote-code \
  --quantization fp8</pre></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.2 SGLang</h3><a id="user-content-62-sglang" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.2 SGLang" href="#62-sglang"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.3 Transformers (Debug / Verification)</h3><a id="user-content-63-transformers-debug--verification" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.3 Transformers (Debug / Verification)" href="#63-transformers-debug--verification"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use this snippet for quick functional verification. For high-throughput serving, use vLLM or SGLang.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer

MODEL_PATH = &quot;&lt;MODEL_PATH_OR_HF_ID&gt;&quot;

# 1. Setup
tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(MODEL_PATH)
model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(
    MODEL_PATH,
    trust_remote_code=True,
    torch_dtype=&quot;auto&quot;,
    device_map=&quot;auto&quot;,
)

# 2. Prepare Input
messages = [{&quot;role&quot;: &quot;user&quot;, &quot;content&quot;: &quot;Explain the significance of the number 42.&quot;}]
inputs = tokenizer.apply_chat_template(
    messages,
    tokenize=True,
    add_generation_prompt=True,
    return_dict=True,
    return_tensors=&quot;pt&quot;,
).to(model.device)

# 3. Generate
generated_ids = model.generate(**inputs, max_new_tokens=128, do_sample=False)
output_text = tokenizer.decode(generated_ids[0][inputs.input_ids.shape[1]:], skip_special_tokens=True)

print(output_text)"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">transformers</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">AutoModelForCausalLM</span>, <span class="pl-v">AutoTokenizer</span>

<span class="pl-c1">MODEL_PATH</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s">"&lt;MODEL_PATH_OR_HF_ID&gt;"</span>

<span class="pl-c"># 1. Setup</span>
<span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">AutoTokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">from_pretrained</span>(<span class="pl-c1">MODEL_PATH</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">model</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-v">AutoModelForCausalLM</span>.<span class="pl-c1">from_pretrained</span>(
    <span class="pl-c1">MODEL_PATH</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">trust_remote_code</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">torch_dtype</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"auto"</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">device_map</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"auto"</span>,
)

<span class="pl-c"># 2. Prepare Input</span>
<span class="pl-s1">messages</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> [{<span class="pl-s">"role"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"user"</span>, <span class="pl-s">"content"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"Explain the significance of the number 42."</span>}]
<span class="pl-s1">inputs</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">apply_chat_template</span>(
    <span class="pl-s1">messages</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">tokenize</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">add_generation_prompt</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">return_dict</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>,
    <span class="pl-s1">return_tensors</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-s">"pt"</span>,
).<span class="pl-c1">to</span>(<span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">device</span>)

<span class="pl-c"># 3. Generate</span>
<span class="pl-s1">generated_ids</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">model</span>.<span class="pl-c1">generate</span>(<span class="pl-c1">**</span><span class="pl-s1">inputs</span>, <span class="pl-s1">max_new_tokens</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">128</span>, <span class="pl-s1">do_sample</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">False</span>)
<span class="pl-s1">output_text</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-s1">tokenizer</span>.<span class="pl-c1">decode</span>(<span class="pl-s1">generated_ids</span>[<span class="pl-c1">0</span>][<span class="pl-s1">inputs</span>.<span class="pl-c1">input_ids</span>.<span class="pl-c1">shape</span>[<span class="pl-c1">1</span>]:], <span class="pl-s1">skip_special_tokens</span><span class="pl-c1">=</span><span class="pl-c1">True</span>)

<span class="pl-en">print</span>(<span class="pl-s1">output_text</span>)</pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">6.4 llama.cpp</h3><a id="user-content-64-llamacpp" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 6.4 llama.cpp" href="#64-llamacpp"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">System Requirements</h4><a id="user-content-system-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: System Requirements" href="#system-requirements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>GGUF Model Weights(int4): 111.5 GB</li>
<li>Runtime Overhead: ~7 GB</li>
<li>Minimum VRAM: 120 GB (e.g., Mac studio, DGX-Spark, AMD Ryzen AI Max+ 395)</li>
<li>Recommended: 128GB unified memory</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Notes</h4><a id="user-content-notes-1" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Notes" href="#notes-1"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>llama.cpp build <strong>must include</strong> <a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/19283" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/19283/hovercard">PR #19283</a></li>
<li>If you encounter tool calling issues on llama.cpp mainline, try building with: <a href="https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/18675" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/ggml-org/llama.cpp/pull/18675/hovercard">PR #18675</a></li>
<li>Community GGUF quantizations: <a href="https://huggingface.co/ubergarm/Step-3.5-Flash-GGUF#quant-collection" rel="nofollow">Step-3.5-Flash Quant Collection</a></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Steps</h4><a id="user-content-steps" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Steps" href="#steps"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Use official llama.cpp</li>
</ol>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto">the folder <code>Step-3.5-Flash/tree/main/llama.cpp</code> is <strong>obsolete</strong></p>
</blockquote>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="git clone https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp
cd llama.cpp"><pre>git clone https://github.com/ggml-org/llama.cpp
<span class="pl-c1">cd</span> llama.cpp</pre></div>
<ol start="2" dir="auto">
<li>Build llama.cpp on Mac:</li>
</ol>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cmake -S . -B build-macos \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DGGML_METAL=ON \
  -DGGML_ACCELERATE=ON \
  -DLLAMA_BUILD_EXAMPLES=ON \
  -DLLAMA_BUILD_COMMON=ON \
  -DGGML_LTO=ON
cmake --build build-macos -j8"><pre>cmake -S <span class="pl-c1">.</span> -B build-macos \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DGGML_METAL=ON \
  -DGGML_ACCELERATE=ON \
  -DLLAMA_BUILD_EXAMPLES=ON \
  -DLLAMA_BUILD_COMMON=ON \
  -DGGML_LTO=ON
cmake --build build-macos -j8</pre></div>
<ol start="3" dir="auto">
<li>Build llama.cpp on DGX-Spark:</li>
</ol>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cmake -S . -B build-cuda \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DGGML_CUDA=ON \
  -DGGML_CUDA_GRAPHS=ON \
  -DLLAMA_CURL=OFF \
  -DLLAMA_BUILD_EXAMPLES=ON \
  -DLLAMA_BUILD_COMMON=ON
cmake --build build-cuda -j8"><pre>cmake -S <span class="pl-c1">.</span> -B build-cuda \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DGGML_CUDA=ON \
  -DGGML_CUDA_GRAPHS=ON \
  -DLLAMA_CURL=OFF \
  -DLLAMA_BUILD_EXAMPLES=ON \
  -DLLAMA_BUILD_COMMON=ON
cmake --build build-cuda -j8</pre></div>
<ol start="4" dir="auto">
<li>Build llama.cpp on AMD Windows:</li>
</ol>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-shell notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="cmake -S . -B build-vulkan \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DLLAMA_CURL=OFF \
  -DGGML_OPENMP=ON \
  -DGGML_VULKAN=ON
cmake --build build-vulkan -j8"><pre>cmake -S <span class="pl-c1">.</span> -B build-vulkan \
  -DCMAKE_BUILD_TYPE=Release \
  -DLLAMA_CURL=OFF \
  -DGGML_OPENMP=ON \
  -DGGML_VULKAN=ON
cmake --build build-vulkan -j8</pre></div>

Using Step 3.5 Flash on Agent Platforms</h2><a id="user-content-7-using-step-35-flash-on-agent-platforms" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 7. Using Step 3.5 Flash on Agent Platforms" href="#7-using-step-35-flash-on-agent-platforms"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">7.1 OpenClaw (Recommended)</h3><a id="user-content-71-openclaw-recommended" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 7.1 OpenClaw (Recommended)" href="#71-openclaw-recommended"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://openclaw.ai" rel="nofollow">OpenClaw</a> is a powerful agentic platform that works seamlessly with Step 3.5 Flash.
Step 3.5 Flash is a perfect fit for OpenClaw due to its high speed and strong agentic capabilities (maintaining deep reasoning and consistency during execution).</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Quick Setup:</strong></p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Install:</strong> <code>curl -fsSL https://openclaw.ai/install.sh | bash</code></li>
<li><strong>Onboard:</strong> Run <code>openclaw onboard</code>.</li>
<li><strong>Configure:</strong> In WebUI (<code>Config</code> -&gt; <code>Models</code>), add a new provider:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Type:</strong> <code>openai-completions</code></li>
<li><strong>Base URL:</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>StepFun:</strong> <code>https://api.stepfun.ai/v1</code> (International) or <code>https://api.stepfun.com/v1</code> (China)</li>
<li><strong>OpenRouter:</strong> <code>https://openrouter.ai/api/v1</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Model ID:</strong> <code>step-3.5-flash</code> (or <code>stepfun/step-3.5-flash</code> for OpenRouter)</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">For a full walkthrough, see our <a href="/stepfun-ai/Step-3.5-Flash/blob/main/cookbooks/openclaw">OpenClaw Cookbook</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">7.2 Claude Code</h3><a id="user-content-72-claude-code" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 7.2 Claude Code" href="#72-claude-code"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can configure <a href="https://docs.anthropic.com/en/docs/agents-and-tools/claude-code/overview" rel="nofollow">Claude Code</a> to use Step 3.5 Flash by modifying your settings.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Update <code>~/.claude/settings.json</code>:</strong></p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{
  &quot;env&quot;: {
    &quot;ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN&quot;: &quot;YOUR_STEPFUN_API_KEY&quot;,
    &quot;ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL&quot;: &quot;https://api.stepfun.ai/&quot;  // Use &quot;https://api.stepfun.com/&quot; for China endpoint
  },
  &quot;model&quot;: &quot;step-3.5-flash&quot;
}"><pre>{
  <span class="pl-ent">"env"</span>: {
    <span class="pl-ent">"ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>YOUR_STEPFUN_API_KEY<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>,
    <span class="pl-ent">"ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>https://api.stepfun.ai/<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>  <span class="pl-ii">// Use "https://api.stepfun.com/" for China endpoint</span>
  },
  <span class="pl-ent">"model"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>step-3.5-flash<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
}</pre></div>
<blockquote>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note:</strong> For more advanced routing or OpenAI-style usage, we recommend <a href="https://github.com/musistudio/claude-code-router">claude-code-router</a>.</p>
</blockquote>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">7.3 OpenAI Codex (CLI)</h3><a id="user-content-73-openai-codex-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 7.3 OpenAI Codex (CLI)" href="#73-openai-codex-cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">To use Step 3.5 Flash with the <code>@openai/codex</code> CLI, update your <code>~/.codex/config.toml</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-toml notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="model=&quot;step-3.5-flash&quot;
model_provider = &quot;stepfun-chat&quot;
preferred_auth_method = &quot;apikey&quot;

[model_providers.stepfun-chat]
name = &quot;OpenAI using response&quot;
base_url = &quot;https://api.stepfun.ai/v1&quot;
env_key = &quot;OPENAI_API_KEY&quot;
wire_api = &quot;chat&quot;"><pre><span class="pl-smi">model</span>=<span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>step-3.5-flash<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">model_provider</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>stepfun-chat<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">preferred_auth_method</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>apikey<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>

[<span class="pl-en">model_providers</span>.<span class="pl-en">stepfun-chat</span>]
<span class="pl-smi">name</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>OpenAI using response<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">base_url</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>https://api.stepfun.ai/v1<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">env_key</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>OPENAI_API_KEY<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>
<span class="pl-smi">wire_api</span> = <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>chat<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></pre></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">7.4 Step-DeepResearch</h3><a id="user-content-74-step-deepresearch" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 7.4 Step-DeepResearch" href="#74-step-deepresearch"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Step 3.5 Flash powers the reasoning core of Step-DeepResearch.
To use it, simply set <code>MODEL_NAME</code> to <code>Step-3.5-Flash</code> in your <code>.env</code> file.</p>
<p dir="auto">See the <a href="https://github.com/stepfun-ai/StepDeepResearch">Step-DeepResearch Repository</a> for full setup instructions.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">9. Known Issues and Future Directions</h2><a id="user-content-9-known-issues-and-future-directions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 9. Known Issues and Future Directions" href="#9-known-issues-and-future-directions"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Token Efficiency</strong>. Step 3.5 Flash achieves frontier-level agentic intelligence but currently relies on longer generation trajectories than Gemini 3.0 Pro to reach comparable quality.</li>
<li><strong>Efficient Universal Mastery</strong>. We aim to unify generalist versatility with deep domain expertise. To achieve this efficiently, we are advancing variants of on-policy distillation, allowing the model to internalize expert behaviors with higher sample efficiency.</li>
<li><strong>RL for More Agentic Tasks</strong>. While Step 3.5 Flash demonstrates competitive performance on academic agentic benchmarks, the next frontier of agentic AI necessitates the application of RL to intricate, expert-level tasks found in professional work, engineering, and research.</li>
<li><strong>Operational Scope and Constraints</strong>. Step 3.5 Flash is tailored for coding and work-centric tasks, but may experience reduced stability during distribution shifts. This typically occurs in highly specialized domains or long-horizon, multi-turn dialogues, where the model may exhibit repetitive reasoning, mixed-language outputs, or inconsistencies in time and identity awareness.</li>
</ol>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">10. Co-Developing the Future</h2><a id="user-content-10-co-developing-the-future" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 10. Co-Developing the Future" href="#10-co-developing-the-future"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">We view our roadmap as a living document, evolving continuously based on real-world usage and developer feedback.
As we work to shape the future of AGI by expanding broad model capabilities, we want to ensure we are solving the right problems. We invite you to be part of this continuous feedback loop—your insights directly influence our priorities.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Join the Conversation</strong>: Our Discord community is the primary hub for brainstorming future architectures, proposing capabilities, and getting early access updates 🚀</li>
<li><strong>Report Friction</strong>: Encountering limitations? You can open an issue on GitHub or flag it directly in our Discord support channels.</li>
</ul>
